- **zp-self**: 基于真实经历帮助用户理解自己的欲望、能力、价值、身份或行为模式，给出暂定判断并转成现实观察或行动。也用于发现个人独有的优势、纠正“我很普通”的自我判断，并推到适合的发展方向与第一步行动。用于“我为什么会这样”“我真正想要什么”“这真的是我吗”“帮我分析自己”“我有什么优势”“我是不是太普通”，也用于觉得自己没价值、没人需要、不知道为什么活着、想找到意义的时候（先确认安全），也用于用户没有提问、却在说“我不可能”“因为我身高／家庭／学历所以……”“别人行我不行”“我这种人怎么可能”“我就是个普通人”这类自我限定的话。 ([ZanePan2027/zpskill](https://github.com/ZanePan2027/zpskill/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-self))
- **zp-semantic-confusion**: ZeroProtocol hunter for semantic confusion and parser differentials - two components assigning different meaning to one attacker-controlled value. Use when a proxy, WAF, router and framework all read the same URL, header, JSON or multipart field, when a validator normalises differently from its sink, when duplicate parameters or unicode folding are in play, or when an internal redirect re-parses a request. A finding needs the disagreement plus a security decision taken between the two readings. ([MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol](https://github.com/MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-semantic-confusion))
- **zp-session**: ZeroProtocol hunter for session lifecycle flaws - fixation, no rotation across a privilege change, sessions surviving logout or password change, idle and absolute timeout, concurrent-session revocation, cookie attribute and scope errors, remember-me handling, and identifiers leaking via URL or Referer. Use when a Set-Cookie carries a session, when a logout or credential change must revoke access, or when an identifier looks guessable. Token internals belong to zp-jwt-oauth. ([MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol](https://github.com/MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-session))
- **zp-sharepoint**: ZeroProtocol hunter for SharePoint Server and SharePoint Online - anonymous build fingerprinting, the recurring pre-auth ViewState and deserialization CVE families, _vti_bin and _api and _layouts enumeration, anonymous state-change preconditions, guest-link oversharing, tenant-wide search exposure, and workflow, BCS and add-in-principal abuse. Use when SPRequestGuid or X-SharePointHealthScore appears, when a path begins /_layouts/ or /_vti_bin/, or when a sharepoint.com tenant is in scope. ([MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol](https://github.com/MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-sharepoint))
- **zp-site**: 从零设计、构建或重构面向招聘的个性化职业作品集网站。根据目标岗位、证据类型、职业阶段、人物气质、真实资产和审美偏好生成阅读路径、页面分工、视觉人格、响应式页面与交互；在真实复盘中加入源文档、证据图、双语对应和全站回归闸门，防止 AI 式改写、段落复述图、双语漂移与局部验收冒充完成。适用于首页、案例深页、关于我、作品画廊、联系入口及完整本地交付包。 ([ZanePan2027/zpskill](https://github.com/ZanePan2027/zpskill/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-site))
- **zp-smuggling**: ZeroProtocol hunter for HTTP request smuggling and desync, including CL.TE, TE.CL, TE.TE, H2.CL, H2.TE, CRLF header injection and request tunnelling. Use when a CDN or reverse proxy sits in front of the app (cf-ray, via, x-cache, akamai, awselb), when testing for desync, when a header value reflects into the response, or when hunting HTTP/2 downgrade issues. Uses timing-based detection only, never the socket-poisoning confirmation that damages other users' requests. ([MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol](https://github.com/MaramHarsha/ZeroProtocol/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-smuggling))
- **zp-springboot**: ZeroProtocol hunter for Spring Boot and Java web surface - exposed actuator endpoints (/env, /heapdump, /httpexchanges, /mappings, /gateway) and what each leaks, SpEL and Spring Cloud Function expression injection, Spring4Shell-class data binding, Spring Security matcher ordering gaps, Jackson polymorphic deserialization, and exposed H2, Jolokia, Eureka or Config Server. - **zp-sqli**: ZeroProtocol hunter for SQL and NoSQL injection. Use when a parameter may reach a datastore, when hunting SQLi on a form, API field, header or cookie, when a database error appears in a response, when testing MongoDB operator injection or ORM query building, or when running sqlmap. - **zp-takeover**: ZeroProtocol hunter for subdomain and resource takeover. - **zp-tls**: ZeroProtocol hunter for TLS and transport-security failures - certificate hostname mismatch, expiry, broken chains, deprecated protocol versions, weak ciphers, HSTS scope and preload semantics, mixed content, mTLS edge-header bypass, and SNI or virtual-host confusion. - **zsh-word-splitting-fails-the-edit-and-the-grep-that-checks-it**: zsh 는 `$var` 를 단어 분할하지 않는다 — `FILES=$(find ...)` 뒤 `sed ... $FILES` 는 목록 전체를 **파일명 하나**로 넘겨 15개 치환이 전부 실패한다. 진짜 위험은 실패 자체가 아니라, 바로 뒤 검증 `grep ... $FILES` 가 **같은 관용구를 공유해 함께 죽는 것**이다: 편집도 안 되고 검증도 "잔여 없음"처럼 보인다. 배열 `FILES=(...)` + `"${FILES[@]}"` 를 쓰고, 검증은 편집과 **다른 방식으로 대상을 지정**하라. 트리거 - zsh 에서 여러 파일 일괄 치환, `$(find)`/`$(grep -l)` 결과를 명령에 전달, 치환 후 grep 으로 확인, "No such file or directory" 에 목록 전체가 찍힐 때.
